[New Yisra Colosseum - Grand Hallway]
A large chandelier, hanging on a very high ceiling, lights up the grand hallway. The floor is made of gray marble with black streaks. The red rock walls are decorated with various ornamental weapons. Two very large mesquite stair cases align both walls going up to the spectator seats. The north wall has a blue and a red portcullis, each with a sign that reads "Gladiators only" above it. There is a door on the east wall. A high arch of columns leads to a hall in the west where the clink of coin and the fast-talking of bookies echoes off the walls.

The area is illuminated.

[New Yisra Colosseum - Grand Hallway]
A large chandelier, hanging on a very high ceiling, lights up the grand hallway. The floor is made of gray marble with black streaks. The red rock walls are decorated with various ornamental weapons. Two very large mesquite stair cases align both walls going up to the spectator seats. The north wall has a blue and a red portcullis, each with a sign that reads "Gladiators only" above it. There is a door on the east wall. A high arch of columns leads to a hall in the west where the clink of coin and the fast-talking of bookies echoes off the walls.

The area is illuminated.

[New Yisra Colosseum - Gambling Hall]
Spectators, gamblers, and patrons from all over the Isle gather in this hall, crowding around bookies and money lenders. Scribes write down the respective bets at the furious pace with which they are spoken of and accepted. The continual sounds of money changing hands and papers ruffling make this area seem almost louder than the arena itself. A large wooden board with pegs holds the place and odds of gladiators, their names written on polished wooden markers and hung from their winning, or losing, positions.

[Red rock Mesa]
The top of the mesa spreads out to the east in a slightly rocky terrain. Fire opal chips have been pounded into fine dust by the hooves of the roaming ibex. Bits of ibex horn are noticeable near the more jagged rocks where they could rub their curling extremities of irritants. Ibex tracks and clipped grass denotes their habitation of this mesa. A landslide of uncut fire opals leads into a wide cavern mouth.

The area is illuminated.

[The Harpy Roost]
Waves of sulphuric air pour out of the rock wall as the mesa cliff's side surrenders to a small cavern entrance. Warm water trickles steadily down the interior of the cavity, light glistening off the metallic red stone of the cavern walls. A pile of small humanoid bones are stacked close to the entry, picked clean of flesh and marred by deep claw gouges. Rough handholds carved into the stone are here

[The Harpy Roost]
The sulphuric gas steaming off a naturally heated pool mixes with the stench of rotting flesh to create an almost visible maelstrom of noxious odor. It is difficult to tell whether the winged siren of the roost use the pool to bathe, as a refuse pit, or both. A bubbling pool is here.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Bjeorn the Galirrim
Obvious exits are: northwest, west, bubbling pool

[The Harpy Roost]
The rock walls soar up to the domed ceiling of the cavern, vermilion flecks casting a dim red glow throughout the chamber. A jagged rock juts out of the wall, proudly displaying a cordon of decaying entrails. The screeching of the bird-women above is joined by a chorus of effervescent liquid coming from the south.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Bjeorn the Galirrim
Obvious exits are: west, east, north

You follow Bjeorn...


[The Harpy Roost]
Light seeps through a hole in the rock chamber's ceiling, darkly luminous rays scattering throughout the chamber in the reflection of the rock's red metallic grains. From a ledge high above, the hideous female face of an angry hag glowers down at any who would dare disturb her rest. Piles of refuse line the base of the walls, a disgusting amalgam of rotted food and humanoid remains.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Bjeorn the Galirrim

[Bubbling Pool]
Bubbles of gas seep forth from a vent at the bottom of the heated natural pool, creating the illusion of a boiling-hot cauldron. The burbling liquid is warm but not uncomfortably so. The half-skeletal remains of a hapless Goblin swims by, its skull cracked open as if dropped from a great height. Looking closer, his hand appears to clutch several large vulture feathers in its lifeless grip.

The area is illuminated.

[Red rock Mesa]
The grass covers over to the eastern edge of the mesa. A short olive tree grows in the thin soil with the grass, competing for space and moisture. The little tree is scarred and stripped of bark in some places from the horns of the ibex. In the meadow to the east, just below the mesa, are Zacotl hunters, carefully separating the useful portions of their kill from the waste.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Bjeorn the Galirrim
Obvious exits are: west, southwest, south

[Red rock Mesa]
The top of the mesa spreads out to the east in a slightly rocky terrain. Fire opal chips have been pounded into fine dust by the hooves of the roaming ibex. Bits of ibex horn are noticeable near the more jagged rocks where they could rub their curling extremities of irritants. Ibex tracks and clipped grass denotes their habitation of this mesa. A landslide of uncut fire opals leads into a wide cavern mouth.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: Bjeorn the Galirrim
Obvious exits are: northwest, west, south, southeast, east, north, landslide

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. [Sanctuary of Eyridane and Morana]
The small path ends as the trees part, revealing a small clearing. Sunlight filters down from above, splashing the vibrant green grass and wildflowers. The noise of the central part of the grove all but fades away in this quiet sanctuary. An altar of stone rests in the center of the small clearing of verdant grass. Tangled vines have grown wild across the hard stone and though no markings can be seen, it is obvious that this is a place sacred to the twin goddesses of nature. Overhead the soft warbling of songbirds blends with the chirping crickets below into a sublime symphony of sounds. Small trinkets of adoration and worship have been left at the base of the altar, each one a symbol of devotion to their beloved gods.

The area is illuminated.

[Sanctuary of Eyridane and Morana]
The small path ends as the trees part, revealing a small clearing. Sunlight filters down from above, splashing the vibrant green grass and wildflowers. The noise of the central part of the grove all but fades away in this quiet sanctuary. An altar of stone rests in the center of the small clearing of verdant grass. Tangled vines have grown wild across the hard stone and though no markings can be seen, it is obvious that this is a place sacred to the twin goddesses of nature. Overhead the soft warbling of songbirds blends with the chirping crickets below into a sublime symphony of sounds. Small trinkets of adoration and worship have been left at the base of the altar, each one a symbol of devotion to their beloved gods.

The area is illuminated.

Through the many, high-arched, steel frame, windows a wooden figure can be seen wearing the latest gentleman's fashion. Grey smoke rises from a single stone chimney.A simple plaque hanging from a twisted wrought iron rod reads, "Regal Fashion". Smooth stone steps lead to the entrance. On the south side of the street a large brick chimney funnels white billowy smoke into the air projecting the aroma of fresh baked goods into the area. Through the large bay window a burly human man can be seen kneading and forming dough and placing his creations into an oven. The heavy mahogany door is propped open seemingly to allow for relief from the ovens intense heat.

The area is illuminated.

[Urda Town Square Center]
The former site of a fierce battle for control of this great city was waged here, and on this site is where it's citizens celebrate that victory. Statues of the fallen heroes, and great leaders of the alliance adorn the tops of grand marble archways leading in all directions. They stand ever vigilant against any unseen enemy. The people of Urda flock here to barter and trade, to converse with friends, or just relax near the fountain and have a game of chess. Benches and tables dot the landscape near the edge of the fountain. Hawkers of all type roll through this area with their wares, hoping to make a few coins. Languages of all races are spoken here. The sheer din of the crowd reverberates throughout the area. Passersby stop to admire the detail of the arches as they enter and exit the city square.

The area is illuminated.

Through the large bay window a burly human man can be seen kneading and forming dough and placing his creations into an oven. The heavy mahogany door is propped open seemingly to allow for relief from the ovens intense heat.

[Urda Town Square Center]

[Wooded Path]
Powerful feet stomp the ground, as a young, virile white-tailed buck strides across the trail, confident that nothing would dare threaten him. The stag arches his neck gracefully, as if his impressive rack of antlers weighed nearly nothing. Thin strips of soft velvet can still be seen on the newer growth, marking him as still young, despite his obvious confidence.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: south, northeast

[Wooded Path]
A large stone sits in the middle of the path, causing the trail to curve around it, hiding the rest of the path from view. Moss and creeping ivy have overtaken the boulder, hiding most of the gray stone from view.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: northwest, southwest

[Wooded Path]
The trees have begun to grow together tightly, making passage impossible but for the most stubborn adventurer. It almost seems as if the forest growth is attempting to make an impassible wall to keep unwanted visitors from entering.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: west, southeast

[Wall of Vines]
Towering trees stand side by side, their branches entangled into a thick snarl. Vines have woven themselves through the gaps of the trees, seeking to stop any unwelcome travelers from continuing down the path. Muffled sounds of a large gathering can be heard coming from the far side of the wall of flora.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: east, slender gap in the wall of trees

[Within the Hedge]
Squeezing through the thick vines and surprisingly pliable trees, the outline of an open glade within a towering copse of trees can be seen. The shadows cast into the hedge make it difficult to discern whether it is day or night and the sounds of habitation can be heard more clearly.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: 1st butterfly
Obvious exits are: small outlet towards a clearing, slender gap in the wall of trees

go o

[Secluded Grove]
Trees have grown into a natural wall, protecting the clearing from any unwanted visitors. Vines have been woven through the tangled branches of the trees, blocking the rest of the forest from view. Soft grass peppered with patches of clover grows underfoot, and a small, brown rabbit nibbles contentedly on the green stalks.  A small group of sturdy Galirrim has gathered around a small spring of crystal clear water. Enchanting scents of cooking food waft through the air from a painted-wood tavern, closely followed by the sound merry laughter.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: 1st fox squirrel, 1st blue jay, 2nd fox squirrel
In this room are: small spring of crystal clear water
Obvious exits are: west, east, painted wooden door, slender gap in the wall of trees, gravel road

[Pathway Through the Trees]
A small gap through the tangled trees allows passage to the quiet haven of the Druids and the Twins' other worshipers. The path is narrow and vines hang low, entangling all but the most wary, or welcome visitors.

The area is illuminated.

Obvious exits are: west, east


[Sanctuary of Eyridane and Morana]
The small path ends as the trees part, revealing a small clearing. Sunlight filters down from above, splashing the vibrant green grass and wildflowers. The noise of the central part of the grove all but fades away in this quiet sanctuary. An altar of stone rests in the center of the small clearing of verdant grass. Tangled vines have grown wild across the hard stone and though no markings can be seen, it is obvious that this is a place sacred to the twin goddesses of nature. Overhead the soft warbling of songbirds blends with the chirping crickets below into a sublime symphony of sounds. Small trinkets of adoration and worship have been left at the base of the altar, each one a symbol of devotion to their beloved gods.

The area is illuminated.

In this room you see: 1st estate knight, 2nd estate knight
In this room are: simple stone altar
Obvious exits are: west, an arch of twisting branches
